Another feature of our lake: there are 276 species of birds around it and 60-70 per cent of the population of the steeptail duck, which is endangered on the Earth's scale -- because the lake is not frozen. The Lake Burdur basin is one of the least rainy places in Turkey. The badness of the lake, which was 857 meters in the 1970s, fell down to 840 metres 50 years later, so the denim of Burdur Lake was less than 17 meters. In the 1970s, water levels were seen to reach the top, but in the following years there was also a periodic decline. The decline in the surface area of our lake is remarkable; the surface area of our lake, 203 square miles in 1990, fell to 146 square miles in 2019. According to the International Ramsar Convention, Lake Burdur 1994, the Ramsar area was also declared a first-degree site in 1998, a site for natural life development in 2006, and an area for national importance. In the time of our governments, investments were made that directly and indirectly affected our Lake Burdur. First, in 2004, the biodegradation plant for Burdur city's waste water was completed, and again in the past months, a 7.5 per cent split into Turkey with an 11.5m-euro loan from Burdur Municipality, ilBANK and the European Investment Bank. In the central district, the military, Çindik, Karaçal Dam, and 10 villages around Burdur Lake. The plain of our Tefenni district, the plain of our Karamanlı district, the closed-door watering system of the village of Belenli province, is built here -- in the closed system. Lake management plans were laid out in 2008-2012. Lake Management Plan 2013-2018 2. The Lake Management Plan was carried out in 2019-2023. To prevent and reduce the vaporization, an RD study was conducted: Four pools were opened at the edge of the lake; a solar panel was placed in one of these pools, and we will complete the results of this RD study on chemical products and the amount of evaporation in the lake's water in the coming years. The works with State Water Affairs and TOKI will be carried out in our nine-and-one counties on Lake Bozchay and Karataş, feeding the lake the most, I hope. Our sugar factory was desalinated in 2013, built into solid waste disposal facilities. In the coming days, this basin plan will be presented to the Water Management Coordination Board, where the implementation of the seven articles set there will be essential work for the rescue of Burdur Lake, which I am working on.

This approach will ensure a more effective determination of the needs and expectations of society and a stronger bond between public resources and public services.
